## Peak Allocation Bytes

Both columns share one pair of atomic counters in the `compare_ffi_memory` bench: Rust allocations via the `#[global_allocator]` tracking wrapper, FFI allocations via `ZSTD_customMem` callbacks that call `System.alloc` / `System.dealloc` directly and manually update the same counters with the libzstd-requested size only. Byte counts are directly comparable cross-side.

## Peak Allocation Bytes

Both columns share one pair of atomic counters in the `compare_ffi_memory` bench: Rust allocations via the `#[global_allocator]` tracking wrapper, FFI allocations via `ZSTD_customMem` callbacks that call `System.alloc` / `System.dealloc` directly and manually update the same counters with the libzstd-requested size only. Byte counts are directly comparable cross-side.

## Peak Allocation Bytes

Both columns share one pair of atomic counters in the `compare_ffi_memory` bench: Rust allocations via the `#[global_allocator]` tracking wrapper, FFI allocations via `ZSTD_customMem` callbacks that call `System.alloc` / `System.dealloc` directly and manually update the same counters with the libzstd-requested size only. Byte counts are directly comparable cross-side.

## Peak Allocation Bytes

Both columns share one pair of atomic counters in the `compare_ffi_memory` bench: Rust allocations via the `#[global_allocator]` tracking wrapper, FFI allocations via `ZSTD_customMem` callbacks that call `System.alloc` / `System.dealloc` directly and manually update the same counters with the libzstd-requested size only. Byte counts are directly comparable cross-side.

| Scenario | Label | Level | Stage | Rust peak alloc | C peak alloc |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | ---: | ---: |

| decodecorpus-z000033 | Repo decode corpus sample | level_-2_fast | decompress-rust_stream | 1024319 | 1117863 |
| decodecorpus-z000033 | Repo decode corpus sample | level_-3_fast | compress | 1146946 | 2147020 |

## Peak Allocation Bytes

Both columns share one pair of atomic counters in the `compare_ffi_memory` bench: Rust allocations via the `#[global_allocator]` tracking wrapper, FFI allocations via `ZSTD_customMem` callbacks that call `System.alloc` / `System.dealloc` directly and manually update the same counters with the libzstd-requested size only. Byte counts are directly comparable cross-side.

## Peak Allocation Bytes

Both columns share one pair of atomic counters in the `compare_ffi_memory` bench: Rust allocations via the `#[global_allocator]` tracking wrapper, FFI allocations via `ZSTD_customMem` callbacks that call `System.alloc` / `System.dealloc` directly and manually update the same counters with the libzstd-requested size only. Byte counts are directly comparable cross-side.

## Peak Allocation Bytes

Both columns share one pair of atomic counters in the `compare_ffi_memory` bench: Rust allocations via the `#[global_allocator]` tracking wrapper, FFI allocations via `ZSTD_customMem` callbacks that call `System.alloc` / `System.dealloc` directly and manually update the same counters with the libzstd-requested size only. Byte counts are directly comparable cross-side.

## Peak Allocation Bytes

Both columns share one pair of atomic counters in the `compare_ffi_memory` bench: Rust allocations via the `#[global_allocator]` tracking wrapper, FFI allocations via `ZSTD_customMem` callbacks that call `System.alloc` / `System.dealloc` directly and manually update the same counters with the libzstd-requested size only. Byte counts are directly comparable cross-side.

## Peak Allocation Bytes

Both columns share one pair of atomic counters in the `compare_ffi_memory` bench: Rust allocations via the `#[global_allocator]` tracking wrapper, FFI allocations via `ZSTD_customMem` callbacks that call `System.alloc` / `System.dealloc` directly and manually update the same counters with the libzstd-requested size only. Byte counts are directly comparable cross-side.

| Scenario | Label | Level | Stage | Rust peak alloc | C peak alloc |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | ---: | ---: |
| decodecorpus-z000033 | Repo decode corpus sample | level_-1_fast | compress | 1144594 | 2113576 |

## Peak Allocation Bytes

Both columns share one pair of atomic counters in the `compare_ffi_memory` bench: Rust allocations via the `#[global_allocator]` tracking wrapper, FFI allocations via `ZSTD_customMem` callbacks that call `System.alloc` / `System.dealloc` directly and manually update the same counters with the libzstd-requested size only. Byte counts are directly comparable cross-side.

## Peak Allocation Bytes

Both columns share one pair of atomic counters in the `compare_ffi_memory` bench: Rust allocations via the `#[global_allocator]` tracking wrapper, FFI allocations via `ZSTD_customMem` callbacks that call `System.alloc` / `System.dealloc` directly and manually update the same counters with the libzstd-requested size only. Byte counts are directly comparable cross-side.

| Scenario | Label | Level | Stage | Rust peak alloc | C peak alloc |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | ---: | ---: |
| decodecorpus-z000033 | Repo decode corpus sample | level_10_lazy | compress | 6335438 | 12972419 |
| decodecorpus-z000033 | Repo decode corpus sample | level_10_lazy | decompress-c_stream | 1090051 | 1118027 |

## Peak Allocation Bytes

Both columns share one pair of atomic counters in the `compare_ffi_memory` bench: Rust allocations via the `#[global_allocator]` tracking wrapper, FFI allocations via `ZSTD_customMem` callbacks that call `System.alloc` / `System.dealloc` directly and manually update the same counters with the libzstd-requested size only. Byte counts are directly comparable cross-side.

## Peak Allocation Bytes

Both columns share one pair of atomic counters in the `compare_ffi_memory` bench: Rust allocations via the `#[global_allocator]` tracking wrapper, FFI allocations via `ZSTD_customMem` callbacks that call `System.alloc` / `System.dealloc` directly and manually update the same counters with the libzstd-requested size only. Byte counts are directly comparable cross-side.

## Peak Allocation Bytes

Both columns share one pair of atomic counters in the `compare_ffi_memory` bench: Rust allocations via the `#[global_allocator]` tracking wrapper, FFI allocations via `ZSTD_customMem` callbacks that call `System.alloc` / `System.dealloc` directly and manually update the same counters with the libzstd-requested size only. Byte counts are directly comparable cross-side.

## Peak Allocation Bytes

Both columns share one pair of atomic counters in the `compare_ffi_memory` bench: Rust allocations via the `#[global_allocator]` tracking wrapper, FFI allocations via `ZSTD_customMem` callbacks that call `System.alloc` / `System.dealloc` directly and manually update the same counters with the libzstd-requested size only. Byte counts are directly comparable cross-side.

## Peak Allocation Bytes

Both columns share one pair of atomic counters in the `compare_ffi_memory` bench: Rust allocations via the `#[global_allocator]` tracking wrapper, FFI allocations via `ZSTD_customMem` callbacks that call `System.alloc` / `System.dealloc` directly and manually update the same counters with the libzstd-requested size only. Byte counts are directly comparable cross-side.

## Peak Allocation Bytes

Both columns share one pair of atomic counters in the `compare_ffi_memory` bench: Rust allocations via the `#[global_allocator]` tracking wrapper, FFI allocations via `ZSTD_customMem` callbacks that call `System.alloc` / `System.dealloc` directly and manually update the same counters with the libzstd-requested size only. Byte counts are directly comparable cross-side.

## Peak Allocation Bytes

Both columns share one pair of atomic counters in the `compare_ffi_memory` bench: Rust allocations via the `#[global_allocator]` tracking wrapper, FFI allocations via `ZSTD_customMem` callbacks that call `System.alloc` / `System.dealloc` directly and manually update the same counters with the libzstd-requested size only. Byte counts are directly comparable cross-side.
